- **biz-product-manager**: Use this agent when you need to make product strategy decisions, prioritize features, or define roadmap plans based on user needs and business goals. ([jpolog/nix-os](https://github.com/jpolog/nix-os/blob/HEAD/pi-agent-data/agent/agents/biz-product-manager.md))
- **biz-project-manager**: Use this agent when you need to establish project plans, track execution progress, manage risks, control budget/schedule, and coordinate stakeholders across complex initiatives. ([jpolog/nix-os](https://github.com/jpolog/nix-os/blob/HEAD/pi-agent-data/agent/agents/biz-project-manager.md))
- **biz-qc-reviewer**: Use this agent to verify any change touching the project's protected business logic — the 6 financial formulas listed in CLAUDE.md Rule 2 and Documents/Reference/PROJECT_CONTEXT.md "Critical Business Logic". Trigger this agent before approving any commit on a `[BIZ-QC-NEEDED]` branch. The agent re-derives each affected formula from the canonical spec and compares it against the new code, returning PASS/FAIL with explicit reasoning. Use proactively whenever a diff touches `backend/routes/budget.py`, `backend/routes/reports.py`, `backend/routes/expenses.py`, or `backend/models.py`. ([RahmanBhuiyan/monthly-budget-planning-rd](https://github.com/RahmanBhuiyan/monthly-budget-planning-rd/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/biz-qc-reviewer.md))
- **biz-quality-specialist**: Business Model & Quality Framework Analysis Agent — deep-dive into pricing, quality gates, metrics, and compliance ([idoforgod/SaaS-AgenticWorkflow](https://github.com/idoforgod/SaaS-AgenticWorkflow/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/biz-quality-specialist.md) · ★37)
- **biz-sales-engineer**: Use this agent when you need to conduct technical pre-sales activities including solution architecture, proof-of-concept development, and technical demonstrations for complex sales deals. ([jpolog/nix-os](https://github.com/jpolog/nix-os/blob/HEAD/pi-agent-data/agent/agents/biz-sales-engineer.md))
- **biz-scrum-master**: Use when teams need facilitation, process optimization, velocity improvement, or agile ceremony management—especially for sprint planning, retrospectives, impediment removal, and scaling agile practices across multiple teams. ([jpolog/nix-os](https://github.com/jpolog/nix-os/blob/HEAD/pi-agent-data/agent/agents/biz-scrum-master.md))
- **biz-strategist**: Use this agent for the business side of a product - choosing a monetization model, pricing, MVP scope cutting, competitor scan, landing page copy, launch plan. Give it the product idea and target user. ([T4LEL/Claude-Arsenal](https://github.com/T4LEL/Claude-Arsenal/blob/HEAD/agents/biz-strategist.md) · ★1)
- **biz-technical-writer**: Use this agent when you need to create, improve, or maintain technical documentation including API references, user guides, SDK documentation, and getting-started guides. ([jpolog/nix-os](https://github.com/jpolog/nix-os/blob/HEAD/pi-agent-data/agent/agents/biz-technical-writer.md))
- **biz-the-briefer**: Business monitoring, operational pulse, and cross-business situational awareness. Use Biz when reviewing the health of active businesses (SurvivorPulse, eCommerce, advisor tool), generating a weekly business brief, tracking key business metrics, or identifying operational flags across Michael's portfolio. Biz monitors the operational layer — Luigi owns the build layer. ([mwolff328-stack/WolffClaude](https://github.com/mwolff328-stack/WolffClaude/blob/HEAD/agents/biz-the-briefer.md) · ★1)
- **biz-ux-researcher**: Use this agent when you need to conduct user research, analyze user behavior, or generate actionable insights to validate design decisions and uncover user needs. - **biz-wordpress-master**: Use this agent when you need to architect, optimize, or troubleshoot WordPress implementations ranging from custom theme/plugin development to enterprise-scale multisite platforms. - **bl-gate**: Reviews a build design against this repo's own laws — its rubric documents, architecture, ownership and the doctrine — BEFORE any code is written. Never rewrites the doc, never edits code. ([zanybu/plot-and-prove](https://github.com/zanybu/plot-and-prove/blob/HEAD/agents/bl-gate.md))
- **bl-init-expert**: Git-branchless initialization specialist for setting up repos with branchless workflow ([jpoutrin/product-forge](https://github.com/jpoutrin/product-forge/blob/HEAD/plugins/git-workflow/agents/bl-init-expert.md) · ★16)
- **bl-integrator**: Owns the SEAMS between branches of a build — the defects no single branch owner can see. Merges branch worktrees, hunts contradictions between them, and runs the whole-build check no individual builder ran. Station ③ᶜ of the buildloop. Exists because "many authors, no integrator" is the root cause behind an entire class of worst-bug. ([zanybu/plot-and-prove](https://github.com/zanybu/plot-and-prove/blob/HEAD/agents/bl-integrator.md))
- **bl-planner**: Legge una issue (o un cluster) e il codice, e produce la scheda a 5 campi da far ratificare all'orchestratore. Non implementa. ([valerielinc-ops/frontaliere-workspace](https://github.com/valerielinc-ops/frontaliere-workspace/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/bl-planner.md))
- **bl-planner**: Writes the build design through the lens of the profession that actually owns this class of problem. Works out what KIND of problem it is, who does it for a living, how those practitioners approach it, and drafts the full build doc in their idiom using the /buildplan format. Station ② of the buildloop — runs after the audit squad, hands its doc to bl-gate. Writes only the build doc; never touches source. ([zanybu/plot-and-prove](https://github.com/zanybu/plot-and-prove/blob/HEAD/agents/bl-planner.md))
- **bl-prover**: Runs an approved build doc's acceptance checks against what was actually built — from a context that never saw the build session's reasoning. Classifies every result HARNESS-PROVEN or LIVE-PROVEN, hunts the four ways a harness lies, and returns a per-check verdict. Station ③ᵇ of the buildloop. Never fixes what it finds, never edits source. ([zanybu/plot-and-prove](https://github.com/zanybu/plot-and-prove/blob/HEAD/agents/bl-prover.md))
- **bl-rootcause**: Takes a list of symptoms and collapses them into the smallest set of real defects. Exists because four separate red findings once turned out to be ONE bug, and because a retrieval problem got four wrong diagnoses before anyone found the corrupt index underneath all of them. Station ① of the buildloop, after bl-scout and bl-runtime report. Never edits. ([zanybu/plot-and-prove](https://github.com/zanybu/plot-and-prove/blob/HEAD/agents/bl-rootcause.md))
- **bl-runtime**: Read-only reality check on what is ACTUALLY running. Compares the config file against the RUNNING process environment, checks service state, deployed version drift, and data-store stats. Exists because config in a file is not config in a process. Station ① of the buildloop. Never restarts, never writes. ([zanybu/plot-and-prove](https://github.com/zanybu/plot-and-prove/blob/HEAD/agents/bl-runtime.md))
- **bl-scout**: Rimisura la claim di una issue del backlog frontaliereticino. Conta, legge un artifact, verifica se il difetto esiste ancora. Non modifica nulla. ([valerielinc-ops/frontaliere-workspace](https://github.com/valerielinc-ops/frontaliere-workspace/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/bl-scout.md))
- **bl-scout**: Read-only mapper for a codebase. Answers "where does X live", "what actually runs on the live path", "what's already been tried" — without polluting the main session's context. - **bloat-resolver**: Applies confirmed bloat removals from /dso:remediate Path B (auto-resolve). Treats its output as final — performs dependency checks before each deletion and emits NO_CHANGE with categorized reasons when removal is unsafe. ([navapbc/digital-service-orchestra](https://github.com/navapbc/digital-service-orchestra/blob/HEAD/plugins/dso/agents/bloat-resolver.md) · ★6)
- **bloat-reviewer**: Reviews a commit for code that is bigger than the problem — long methods, deep nesting, speculative abstraction, unused parameters, dead code. Use when reviewing a diff for bloat, complexity, or over-engineering. ([galilei2050/belay](https://github.com/galilei2050/belay/blob/HEAD/plugins/review-panel/agents/bloat-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **bloat-reviewer**: Reviews a change in the Curio repo for code it doesn't need - abstractions with one caller, defensive try/catch blocks and checks that can't fire, helpers that duplicate existing ones, pass-through wrappers, options nobody uses, dead code and dependencies a few lines would replace - and says what the simpler version would be. Use before finishing every task, alongside the spec and security reviewers. Read-only: it reports findings and changes nothing. ([josh098900/curio](https://github.com/josh098900/curio/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/bloat-reviewer.md))
- **bloat-scout**: Discovers candidate tech debt signals (bloat, dead code, over-abstraction) from target codebase with configurable lens ([rp1-run/rp1](https://github.com/rp1-run/rp1/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/agents/bloat-scout.md) · ★38)
